Maybe transform by reversing half: Let split a into first half and second half. Self-convolution center+ corresponds to cross-convolution of first half with reversed second half. Specifically for k around n-1, c_k = Σ_i a_i a_{k-i} with i in first half, k-i in second. Let u_i = a_i for i=0..m-1, v_j=a_{n-1-j} for j=0..m-1 (reversed second half). Then c_{n-1+s} ≈ (u * v)_{?}. For even n, central region is convolution of two half sequences. To make all central coefficients low, we need two nonnegative sequences u,v with low cross-convolution peak, while endpoint regions (u*u, v*v) also low. This decomposition may help. Projected gradient with LSE:
```
def pg_smooth(a, tau, iters, deadline):
  for:
    c = conv_fft(a)
    w = softmax(c/tau)
    D = max c + tau logsumexp
    g = grad_weights(w,a)
    # Project gradient onto sum-zero: g = g - np.mean(g) (to preserve sum before projection)
    # Actually if we renormalize after, projection not necessary but helps.
    for eta in [0.2,0.5,1,2,4,8,16,32]:
       a2 = np.maximum(a - eta*g,0); s=a2.sum(); if s>0: a2/=s
       D2 = smooth_val(conv_fft(a2),tau) or exact max? Use exact score to pick.
    if best<current: a=a2
    update tau
```

  c = param[:K]

  z = S.T @ c  # shape n

```
def local_score(a):
   n=len(a); s=a.sum(); if s < 0.01: return inf
   c=conv(a); maxc=c.max()
   return float(2*n*maxc/(s*s))

Let's see if previous SOTA LP can be improved by using **exact active-set solving via projected quadratic**. At local optimum, active weights w satisfy G_i=(a⋆w)_i constant on support. We can solve for w over a set of active lags such that G flat. This is a linear least squares: minimize variance of H_i=(a⋆w)_i subject w≥0, sum w=1. a is fixed; H = T_w? For given w, H_i = Σ_{k} w_k a_{k-i}. We can express H = (a * w) as convolution (correlation). We can solve least squares for w to make H constant vector 1 over i. This is a linear system with Toeplitz: For each i, Σ_{k} a_{k-i} w_k = 1. This is n equations, 2n-1 unknowns. We can find nonnegative w? The dual LP exactly! This is the same covering LP. We can solve least-squares with nonnegative constraints maybe via NNLS. If we find w with H≈1, then objective derivative zero (for current active weights? But w not restricted to active? It is distribution over lags; if H constant and c_k≤M for support of w, then KKT satisfied for f(a) if w only on active set with c_k=M? Need w support active and c_w =M. This is similar to LP but we can approximate.

This suggests an iteration:
1. Given a, let active set K be all lags with c_k near max (within tolerance). We want choose nonnegative weights w supported on K such that H_i = a⋆w_i is as flat as possible (preferably constant) to satisfy KKT. Solve a constrained least squares: minimize ||H - mean(H) 1||^2 subject w≥0, sum w=1 over K. If we find w, then gradient G=2H has zero projection onto sum constraint. The current a is stationary for the weighted objective; but if inactive constraints slack, max may not decrease? Need also ensure all c≤M and weights only active. Can we use previous SOTA LP idea without full LP: **active set linear projection**?
At current a with M=max c. Solve primal? The LP max sum b subject to a*b ≤M, b≥0. We can solve the dual covering LP via **multiplicative weights** faster and produce b. Let's attempt to implement a lightweight dual update to generate a new point b (or update a).
Dual: minimize M * Σ y_k subject to H_i = Σ_k a_{k-i} y_k ≥ 1, y_k ≥0. Since H linear in y. Let scale y so min Σ. class FourierState:
    def __init__(self, n, K):
        self.n=n; self.K=K; self.tau=1.0/n
        i=np.arange(n,n,dtype=float)
        ms=np.arange(1,K+1,dtype=float)
        # To reduce memory, compute S in chunks? K*n okay.
        self.S = np.sin(2*np.pi*ms[:,None]*i[None,:]/n)
        self.N = _next_pow2(3*n-2) # for grad
        self.Ssum? sums zero but floating maybe small. We can nullify mean by subtracting column means to ensure sum zero: self.S -= self.S.mean(axis=1, keepdims=True)
```

`lowdim_a(x,state)`:

K=state.K; c=x[:K]; alpha=float(x[K])
z = (c[:,None]*state.S).sum(axis=0)  # K*n, but use state.S @ c? state.S shape K,n; z=state.S.T @ c? Actually S[m,i]; z_i=Σ S[m,i] c_m = S.T @ c.

Jacobian:
```
def lowdim_jac(x):
   state=_LOW_STATE
   a=lowdim_a(x,state)
   c=_convolve_fft(a)
   val,w=_smooth_value_and_weights(c,state.tau)
   grad_a=2*_grad_weights(w,a)
   P = a? Wait P=sum p? a=p/P. We need P = sum p, but we didn't store. Compute p again or store from lowdim_a? We can in lowdim_a return a,p,z? For jac, recompute z,p.
   P = p.sum()
   dot = np.dot(grad_a, p)  # sum GA_i p_i
   grad_p = grad_a / P - dot / (P*P)
   # tanh and sech2
   t = np.tanh(z)
   sech2 = 1 - t*t
   alpha=...
   # grad alpha = dot(grad_p, t)
   # grad c_m = alpha * dot(grad_p, sech2 * S_m)
   # To avoid storing S? state.S.
   weight = grad_p * (alpha * sech2)  (for c) shape n
   grad_c = state.S @ weight? Since S[m,i], dot over i: S @ weight.
   grad_alpha = np.dot(grad_p, t)
   out = np.empty(K+1); out[:K]=grad_c; out[K]=grad_alpha
   return out
```

Implementation of FFT convolution efficient:
```
def _next_pow2(m):
    if m <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
```
`convolve_fft(a)`:
```
n=len(a); L=2*n-1; N=_next_pow2(L)
fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)[:L]
# due to numerical noise, set tiny negative to 0 if all near nonnegative
# But if c has negative from numerical only, max unaffected. Use c=np.where(c<0 and >-1e-12,0,c).
return c
```
Since a nonnegative, c nonnegative mathematically. Squaring FFT (not using conjugate) gives real; imaginary due error. `irfft` yields real. Coeffs can be small negative if a has tiny negative? We clip.
Need for n=1? handle.

`grad_weights(w,a)`:

```
n=len(a); L=2*n-1; N=_next_pow2(3*n-2) # but if N for conv same 2n maybe 3n-2 <=? For n=4806, 2n=9612? Wait full conv of a(a) length 9611 needs N>=9611. grad conv length 3n-2 =14410, needs N>=14410. Next pow2 16384, while conv N 16384 also (2n=9612 next 16384), so same. For larger n, conv N maybe >? n=8000: 2n=16000 next 32768; 3n=24000 next 32768; same. Okay may be same. We can use N=max(_next_pow2(2n-1), _next_pow2(3n-2)).
wr = w[::-1]
fa = rfft(a,N); fw=rfft(wr,N); q=irfft(fa*fw,N)
return q[2*n-2 - np.arange(n)]   # shape n
```

a=[a0,a1], w=[w0,w1,w2]. g0 = a0 w0 + a1 w1. wr=[w2,w1,w0]. conv q:

Polish smooth line search:

def smooth_projected_polish(a, max_time, tau0_dim, stop_decrease=1e-12):
  n=len(a); a=a.copy(); s=a.sum(); if s>0: a/=s
  best_score=local_score(a); best_a=a.copy()
  eta=1.0
  for tau_dim in [0.5,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005]:
     tau=tau_dim/n
     # up to time slice
     while time.monotonic()<deadline and eta>...:
        c=conv(a); val,w=smooth(c,tau); g=2*grad_weights(w,a); g-=g.mean()
        # choose candidate: try eta_current and maybe 2*eta
        Dbest=val; abest=a
        for factor in [0.5,1,2]:
           t=eta*factor
           a2=np.maximum(a - t*g,0); s2=a2.sum()
           if s2>1e-15: a2/=s2
           c2=conv(a2); D2,_=smooth(c2,tau)
           if D2 < Dbest - 1e-15: Dbest=D2; abest=a2
        if Dbest < val - 1e-15:
           a=abest; eta*=1.2? Reset? 
           score=local_score(a)
           if score<best_score: best...
        else:
           eta*=0.5
           if eta<1e-3: break
     # reduce tau
```
This does 4 conv per iter (current+3 candidates) if factors 3. Could use 1 candidate. To reduce cost, use factors [1] and if no improvement halve eta. Use current conv to compute val; candidate conv one. We'll do:

  a2...
  D2...
  if D2 < val: accept break
  else t*=0.5
```
So 2-7 conv. Accept only if smooth improvement. If no, after max reduce eta. Use time.

def full_lbfgs(a0, max_time):
 n=len(a0); x=np.log(a0+1e-12); best_a=a0; best_score=local_score(a0); deadline=...

    x=result.x

def softmax(x):

Now let's test mathematical formulas with a simple candidate to ensure gradient sign correct. Objective D=smooth max(c). `grad_a_i = 2∑_j a_j w_{i+j}`. If we do `a_new = a - eta*grad_a` with sum constraint projection, should decrease weighted sum. In softmax parameterization, grad_x = a*(grad_a - dot). This is gradient of D wrt x. Since D depends on a=softmax(x). Standard derivative: ∂D/∂x_i = a_i(∂D/∂a_i - Σ_j a_j ∂D/∂a_j). Yes. In constrained sum, projection (subtract dot) ensures sum grad_x = -Σ a_i^2 grad_a? Actually sum grad_x = Σ a_i grad_a_i - (Σ a_i)Σ a_i grad_a_i =0? Wait formula gives Σ grad_x = Σ a_i g_i - (Σ a_i)Σ a_i g_i =0. This is projection onto tangent of log? Good.

For lowdim raw p normalized, gradient formula should align with lowdim a. Since objective scaled n. Good.

One nuance: In lowdim, p normalized by P=Psum. If p≥0 but can be zero, derivative a_i=p_i/P. The gradient of normalization projection different from softmax: For fixed P, if adding constant to p doesn't scale? Our formula works.

`_FourierState` class:
```
class _FourierState:
    def __init__(self,n,K):
       self.n=n; self.K=K; self.tau=1.0
       i=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
       ms=np.arange(1,K+1,dtype=float)
       self.S=np.sin(2.0*np.pi*ms[:,None]*i[None,:]/n)
       self.S -= self.S.mean(axis=1, keepdims=True)
```
Could memory for K large. Good.

def _ensure_sum_one(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0.0)
   a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
   s=a.sum()
   if s<=1e-15: return np.ones(len(a))/len(a)
   return a/s
```

Conv FFT performance: Use real FFT. Need ensure `N` power of two; for small n, 1. Use `np.fft.rfft(a, N=N)`. If a is not contiguous? okay.
```
def _conv_fft(a):
    n=len(a); L=2*n-1
    N=1 << (L-1).bit_length() if L>1 else 1
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, N=N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, n=N)[:L]
    # small negatives to zero
    if c.min() < 0:
       # if min > -1e-9, set; else keep? But math nonneg; due numerical could -1e-12.
       c=np.where(c<0, 0.0, c)
    return c
```
If clipping negative due true? a nonnegative, true no negative. Good.
But if using lowdim a positive, okay.

`_grad_weights(w,a)`:
```
def _grad_weights(w,a):
   n=len(a); L=2*n-1
   need = 3*n-2
   N=1 << (max(L, need)-1).bit_length() if max>1 else 1
   wr=w[::-1]
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a, N=N)
   fw=np.fft.rfft(wr, N=N)
   q=np.fft.irfft(fa*fw, n=N)
   idx = 2*n - 2 - np.arange(n, dtype=int)
   return q[idx]
```
If n=1, need=1? L=1, need=1. idx [0]. Works. `w` length should be 2n-1. If not, adjust? Use `w=np.asarray(w); if len(w)!=2*n-1: ...` Not.

`_smooth_val_weights(c,tau)`:
```
def _smooth_val_weights(c,tau):
   m=float(c.max())
   if tau <= 0:
      w=np.zeros_like(c); w[np.argmax(c)]=1.0; return m,w
   sh=(c-m)/tau
   ex=np.exp(sh)
   s=ex.sum()
   w=ex/s
   return m + tau*np.log(s), w
```
To avoid underflow if tau very small and c-m big negative; exp min underflows to 0, okay, s≥1 (because max term 1). Use tau min maybe 1e-6*n; c-m maybe -0.5*1/n? sh ~ -500 for tau 0.0001/n? okay exp underflow. Fine.

`_full_jac`:
```
def _full_jac(x):
   st=_FULL_STATE
   a=_softmax(x)
   c=_conv_fft(a)
   val,w=_smooth_val_weights(c, st.tau)
   ga=2.0*_grad_weights(w,a)
   dot=np.dot(a,ga)
   return st.n*a*(ga-dot)
```

Softmax:
```
def _softmax(x):
   y=x-x.max()
   e=np.exp(y)
   s=e.sum()
   if s ==0 or not np.isfinite(s): return np.full_like(x,1.0/len(x), dtype=float)
   return e/s
```
If x contains nonfinite? L-BFGS may produce nan; convert: `x=np.where(np.isfinite(x),x,0)`. Add in fun/jac? Could.

def _lowdim_a(x, st=None):
   if st is None: st=_LOW_STATE
   c=x[:st.K]

   z=np.tanh(st.S.T @ c) ??? Wait tanh requires linear; if using tanh, z should be dot before tanh. To compute p, need `u=st.S.T @ c`; `t=np.tanh(u)`; p=1+alpha*t.
   p=1.0 + alpha*t
   # p positive; maybe small negative if alpha>1? clip.
   if (p<0).any(): p=np.maximum(p,0)
   s=p.sum()

Jacobian:
```
def _lowdim_fun(x):
   st=_LOW_STATE
   a=_lowdim_a(x,st)
   c=_conv_fft(a)
   val,w=_smooth_val_weights(c, st.tau)
   return float(st.n*val)
def _lowdim_jac(x):
   st=_LOW_STATE
   K=st.K
   c=x[:K]
   alpha=float(np.clip(x[K],0,1))
   u=st.S.T @ c
   t=np.tanh(u)
   p=1.0 + alpha*t
   p=np.maximum(p,0.0)
   P=p.sum()
   a=p/P
   cconv=_conv_fft(a)
   val,w=_smooth_val_weights(cconv, st.tau)
   ga=2.0*_grad_weights(w,a)
   dot=np.dot(ga,p)
   grad_p = ga/P - dot/(P*P)
   out=np.empty(K+1)
   if alpha > 0:
       sech2=1.0 - t*t
       weight=grad_p*(alpha*sech2)
       out[:K]=st.S @ weight
   else:
       out[:K]=0
   out[K]=np.dot(grad_p, t)
   out *= st.n
   # replace nan/inf with 0
   out[~np.isfinite(out)] =0
   return out
```
Potential issue: if p clipped at 0, derivative of p wrt c where p=0? Our p formula can be negative if alpha*t < -1; with alpha≤1 and tanh∈[-1,1], p≥0 exactly, no clip needed except floating. If alpha=1 and t=-1 exactly, p=-tiny? clip. Derivative ignoring clip: at p=0, sech2=0 if t=-1, so derivative 0; okay. If clipping due tiny negative, sech2 positive maybe but p zero; derivative not exact but small.
`st.S @ weight` works: S shape (K,n), weight (n,) -> (K,). Good.

Bounds for lowdim: `bounds=[(-6.0,6.0)]*K + [(0.05,0.999)]`. If K=64, list okay. We can allow c ±8. But large c and tanh saturates; no issue. Init maybe c amplitude 1.

Lowdim inits generator:
```
def _lowdim_initializations(K, rng):
   inits=[]
   # single modes
   for f in [1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8,10,13,17,21,29,37,47,53,64]:
       if f<=K:
          for amp in [0.3,0.6,1.0,1.5,2.0]:
             x=np.zeros(K+1); x[f-1]=amp; x[K]=0.6
             inits.append(x)
   # random lowpass
   ms=np.arange(1,K+1)
   decay=1.0/np.sqrt(ms)
   for _ in range(20):
      c=rng.standard_normal(K)*decay*rng.uniform(0.5,2.0)
      alpha=rng.uniform(0.3,0.9)
      x=np.concatenate([c,[alpha]])
      inits.append(x)
   # random signs few modes
   for _ in range(10):
      c=np.zeros(K); k=int(rng.integers(3, min(12,K)+1)); idx=rng.choice(K,k,replace=False); c[idx]=rng.choice([-1,1],k)*rng.uniform(0.5,1.5)
      alpha=rng.uniform(0.4,0.95)
      inits.append(...)
   return inits
```
Need avoid too many; maybe 80. Lowdim search time limits.

`_lowdim_search`:
```
def _lowdim_search(n, K, deadline, seed, max_starts=80):
   global _LOW_STATE, _TIME_STOP_DEADLINE
   st=_FourierState(n,K); _LOW_STATE=st
   rng=default_rng(seed)
   inits=_lowdim_initializations(K,rng)
   best_a=None; best_score=inf
   bounds=[(-8,8)]*K + [(0.05,0.999)]
   tau_dims=[4.0,2.0,1.0,0.5,0.25,0.12,0.06,0.03]
   alloc_end = deadline # search until alloc
   total_started=0
   for x0 in inits:
      if time.monotonic() > deadline - 120: break
      # time per init? we can do many starts but each tau bounded 25s
      for td in tau_dims:
         now=time.monotonic()
         if now > deadline - 60: break
         st.tau=td/n
         # stage budget based on remaining count remaining starts/tau? simple 30s

         if rem<15: break

def _lowdim_search(n, K, deadline, seed, max_starts=None):
    global _LOW_STATE, _TIME_STOP_DEADLINE
    st=_FourierState(n,K); _LOW_STATE=st
    if max_starts is None: max_starts=min(32, max(8, int(deadline - time.monotonic())//15))
    inits=_lowdim_initializations(K, np.random.default_rng(seed))
    # limit inits to max_starts
    inits=inits[:max_starts]
    top=[]
    best_score=inf
    bounds=[(-8.0,8.0) for _ in range(K)] + [(0.05,0.999)]
    taus=[4.0,2.0,1.0,0.5,0.25,0.12,0.06,0.03]
    total_runs=len(inits)*len(taus)
    for idx, x0 in enumerate(inits):
       for j, td in enumerate(taus):
          now=time.monotonic()
          remaining=deadline-now-20
          if remaining <= 0: break
          rem_runs = (len(inits)-idx)*len(taus)-j
          stage = remaining / max(1, rem_runs)
          stage = max(5.0, min(45.0, stage))
          if stage > remaining: stage = min(45.0, max(5.0, remaining*0.9))
          _TIME_STOP_DEADLINE = now + stage
          try:
             res=minimize(_lowdim_fun, x0, jac=_lowdim_jac, bounds=bounds, method='L-BFGS-B',
                          options={'maxiter':500,'maxfun':1000,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-8},
                          callback=_time_callback)
             if res.x is not None: x0=res.x
          except Exception:
             pass
          # after run, evaluate
          a=_lowdim_a(np.asarray(x0,float), st)
          score=_local_score(a)
          if np.isfinite(score):
             if score < best_score:
                best_score=score
             # update top:
             inserted=False
             for t in top:
                if score < t[0]: top.insert(0,(score,a.copy())); inserted=True; break
             if not inserted and len(top)<5: top.append((score,a.copy()))
             if len(top)>5: top.pop()
             print("lowdim init/j ...")
       # if best_score close to 1.5 maybe continue? maybe break if <1.5005? But could improve; continue if time.
    return top
```

Active mix polish function:
```
def _active_mix_polish(a, deadline):
   global _TIME_STOP_DEADLINE? no minimize.
   a=_normalize_nonneg(a)
   best=a.copy(); best_score=_local_score(a)
   n=len(a)
   rng=default_rng(777)
   taus=[2.0,1.0,0.5,0.25,0.12,0.06,0.03]
   for td in taus:
      if time.monotonic()>deadline-5: break
      tau=td/n
      improved_count=0
      for it in range(12):
         now=time.monotonic()
         if now>deadline-5: break
         c=_conv_fft(a)
         val,w=_smooth_val_weights(c,tau)
         H=_grad_weights(w,a)
         # candidates
         cands=[]
         hmin=float(H.min()); hmax=float(H.max()); rngw=max(1e-15,hmax-hmin)
         for frac in (0.1,0.25,0.5):
            thr=hmin+frac*rngw
            b=(H<=thr).astype(float); s=b.sum(); if s>0: b/=s; cands.append(b)
         inv=1.0/(H+1e-12); inv/=inv.sum(); cands.append(inv)
         diff=(hmax-H); diff=np.maximum(diff,0); s=diff.sum(); if s>0: diff/=s; cands.append(diff)
         # random low set: take indices with lowest q maybe
         q=int(min(n, max(10, n//10)))
         idx=np.argpartition(H, q)[:q] if q<n else np.arange(n)
         b=np.zeros(n); b[idx]=1; b/=n/q? already. cands.append(b)
         improved=False
         for b in cands:
            for mix in (0.1,0.3,0.6):
               if time>deadline-5: break
               a2=(1-mix)*a + mix*b
               a2=np.maximum(a2,0); s=a2.sum(); if s<=0: continue; a2/=s
               sc=_local_score(a2)
               if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                   best_score=sc; best=a2.copy(); a=a2.copy(); improved=True; improved_count+=1; break
            if improved: break
         if not improved:
            if improved_count==0 and it>2: break
      print
   return best,best_score
```

t=np.tanh(u)

p=np.maximum(praw,0.0)
P=p.sum()
...

dp_dc = scale*sech2
dp_ds = t
# For clipped points, dp should be 0:
dp_dc_masked = np.where(pos, dp_dc, 0.0)
dp_ds_masked = np.where(pos, dp_ds, 0.0)
weight = grad_p * (dp_dc_masked)
# But dp_dc depends on m? same scalar for all m times S? Actually p_raw derivative wrt c_m = scale*sech2*S_m; yes scalar w_i for each column, so weight_i=grad_p_i*scale*sech2_i if pos else 0. Good.
grad_c = S @ weight # with weight includes scale.
grad_scale = np.dot(grad_p[pos], t[pos])  # no scale factor.
```

Let's detail lowdim inits with scale parameter:
```
def _lowdim_initializations(K, rng):
    inits=[]
    ms=np.arange(1,K+1)
    decays=1.0/np.sqrt(ms)
    modes=[1,2,3,5,7,11,17,23,29,37,47,53,64]
    for f in modes:
       if f<=K:
          for amp in [0.5,1.0,1.5,2.0]:
             for sc in [0.5,1.0,1.8]:
                x=np.zeros(K+1); x[f-1]=amp; x[K]=sc; inits.append(x)
    # random lowpass
    for _ in range(24):
       c=rng.standard_normal(K)*decays*rng.uniform(0.5,2.0)
       x=np.concatenate([c,[rng.uniform(0.5,2.0)]])
       inits.append(x)
    # few mode random
    for _ in range(12):
       c=np.zeros(K)
       k=int(rng.integers(3, min(15,K+1)))
       idx=rng.choice(K,k,replace=False)
       c[idx]=rng.choice([-1,1],size=k)*rng.uniform(0.5,1.5)
       x=... scale rng.uniform(0.5,2.0)
       inits.append(x)
    rng.shuffle(inits)
    return inits
```
This list large ~ (13*4*3)=156 +36=192; max_starts 20. Shuffle to mix. Good.

       arr=np.minimum(arr,1000.0)
       s=arr.sum()
       if s>0.01:
          return arr/s
    return None

Now code skeleton and details.

Let's draft final code mentally:
```
"""..."""
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_TIME_STOP_DEADLINE = None
_FULL_STATE = None
_LOW_STATE = None

def _time_callback(*args):
    dl=_TIME_STOP_DEADLINE
    if dl is None: return False
    return time.monotonic() >= dl

def _png(m):
    if m <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()

def _softmax(x):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=float)
    x=np.where(np.isfinite(x),x,0.0)
    if x.size==0: return x
    y=x-x.max()
    e=np.exp(y)
    s=e.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0:
       return np.ones_like(x)/x.size
    return e/s

def _conv_fft(a, N=None):
    n=a.size
    L=2*n-1
    if N is None: N=_png(L)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, N=N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, n=N)[:L]
    if c.size and c.min() < 0.0:
        c = np.where(c < 0.0, 0.0, c)
    return c

def _grad_weights(w, a, N=None):
    n=a.size
    if N is None: N=_png(3*n-2)
    wr = w[::-1]
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, N=N)
    fw=np.fft.rfft(wr, N=N)
    q=np.fft.irfft(fa*fw, n=N)
    idx=np.arange(2*n-2, n-2, -1) # length n? For n=4806, arange(9610,4803,-1) gives 9610..4804 length4807? Wait!
```
Careful: We need indices `2n-2 - np.arange(n)`: for i=0 index 2n-2; i=n-1 index n-1. Using `np.arange(2*n-2, n-2, -1)` yields start 2n-2, stop n-2 exclusive, step -1: end n-1. Length (2n-2 - (n-1))/1 +1? Example n=3: start4 stop1 step -1 => 4,3,2 (length3) good. For n=1: start0 stop -1 step -1? `np.arange(0, -1, -1)` => [0] good. So use `idx = np.arange(2*n-2, n-2, -1)` works.
But if n large, dtype int.
```
    return q[idx]
```
Check n=2: start2 stop0 => [2,1] length2. Good.

`_smooth_val_weights(c,tau)`:
```
def _smooth_val_weights(c,tau):
    m=float(np.max(c))
    if tau <= 1e-300:
       w=np.zeros_like(c); w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0; return m,w
    sh=(c-m)/tau
    ex=np.exp(sh)
    s=ex.sum()
    w=ex/s
    return m + tau*float(np.log(s)), w
```
If c empty? n>=1. If s=0 impossible due max.

`_local_score(a)`:
```
def _local_score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    if a.size==0: return float('inf')
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0.0)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    a=np.minimum(a,1000.0)
    s=float(np.sum(a))
    if s < 0.01:
       return float('inf')
    n=a.size
    c=_conv_fft(a)
    maxc=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if maxc < 0: maxc=0
    return float(2.0*n*maxc/(s*s))
```
This clamps each to 1000 as official. But during optimization values <1; okay. If values large base, clamping could alter score vs local normalized? If base sum/scale with values >1000? Official clamps. We should mimic. But for optimization, if a has values near 1000, local okay. If base values huge, clamped. Use this for local candidate eval.

But if a sum1 values <1, clamp no effect. For active mix, a sum1. Good.

`_normalize(a)`:
```
def _normalize(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float); a=np.where(finite,0); a=np.maximum(0,a); s=sum; if s<=... return None; return a/s
```

State classes:
```
class _FullState:
   def __init__(self,n):
      self.n=n; self.tau=1.0/n; self.cache_x=None; self.cache_valid=False; self.cache_val=float('inf'); self.cache_grad=None

class _FourierState:
   def __init__(self,n,K):
      self.n=n; self.K=K; self.tau=1.0/n
      i=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
      ms=np.arange(1,K+1,dtype=float)
      self.S=np.sin(2.0*np.pi*ms[:,None]*i[None,:]/float(n))
      self.S -= self.S.mean(axis=1, keepdims=True)
      self.cache_x=None; self.cache_valid=False; self.cache_val=inf; self.cache_grad=None
```
For n large, S memory. Good.

Compute functions:
```
def _full_compute(st,x):
    if st.cache_x is x and st.cache_valid:
        return st.cache_val, st.cache_grad
    a=_softmax(x)
    n=a.size; Nc=_png(2*n-1); Ng=_png(3*n-2)
    c=_conv_fft(a,Nc)
    val,w=_smooth_val_weights(c,st.tau)
    ga=2.0*_grad_weights(w,a,Ng)
    dot=float(np.dot(a,ga))
    gx=st.n*a*(ga-dot)
    gx[~np.isfinite(gx)]=0.0
    st.cache_x=x; st.cache_valid=True; st.cache_val=float(st.n*val); st.cache_grad=gx
    return st.cache_val, gx
```
Need if x modified? fine.

Full fun/jac:
```
def _full_fun(x):
   st=_FULL_STATE; if st is None: return 0.0
   val, gx = _full_compute(st,x)
   return val
def _full_jac(x):
   st=_FULL_STATE; ... val,gx=_full_compute(st,x); return gx
```

Lowdim compute:
```
def _lowdim_compute(st,x):
   if st.cache_x is x and st.cache_valid: return st.cache_val, st.cache_grad
   x=np.where(np.isfinite(x),x,0.0)
   K=st.K; n=st.n
   c=x[:K]
   scale=float(np.clip(x[K],0.0,4.0))
   u=st.S.T @ c
   t=np.tanh(u)
   praw=1.0 + scale*t
   p=np.maximum(praw,0.0)
   P=float(p.sum())
   if P <= 1e-15:
       a=np.ones(n)/n
   else:
       a=p/P
   Nc=_png(2*n-1); Ng=_png(3*n-2)
   cconv=_conv_fft(a,Nc)
   val,w=_smooth_val_weights(cconv, st.tau)
   ga=2.0*_grad_weights(w,a,Ng)
   # dot ga p
   dot=float(np.dot(ga,p)) if a.size else 0.0
   grad_p=ga/P - dot/(P*P)
   out=np.empty(K+1,dtype=float)
   pos=p>1e-14
   if scale > 0:
       weight=grad_p * (scale*(1.0 - t*t))
       weight[~pos]=0.0
       out[:K]=st.S @ weight
   else:
       out[:K]=0.0
   out[K]=float(np.dot(grad_p[pos], t[pos])) if np.any(pos) else 0.0
   out *= st.n
   out[~np.isfinite(out)]=0.0
   st.cache_x=x; st.cache_valid=True; st.cache_val=float(st.n*val); st.cache_grad=out
   return st.cache_val, out
```
If scale bound lower 0.05; x may out. If pos all false? P=0 handled a uniform, but grad_p computed with P? If P tiny, P can be 0? If all praw negative, P=0, set a uniform; but grad? P=0 leads division by zero. If P<=1e-15, set out zeros? Better:
```
if P <=1e-12: ... uniform; grad zeros
```
But with scale≤4 and sine mean zero, P not tiny. Still handle:
```
if P <= 1e-12:
   a=...; out=zeros; cache...
```
Implement.

       if score < s:
          top.insert(i,(score,seq? actually current a.copy())); ins=True; break

       top.append((score,a.copy()))
    if len(top)>maxlen: top.pop()

```
def _active_mix_polish(a, deadline):
   a=_normalize(a)
   if a is None: return a best? uniform
   best=a.copy(); best_score=_local_score(a)
   n=len(a); rng=np.random.default_rng(987654)
   for td in [3.0,1.5,0.75,0.375,0.1875,0.09375]:
      tau=td/n
      improved_tau=0
      for it in range(10):
         now=time.monotonic(); if now>deadline-3: break
         c=_conv_fft(a)
         val,w=_smooth_val_weights(c,tau)
         H=_grad_weights(w,a)
         if not np.all(np.isfinite(H)): break
         hmin=float(np.min(H)); hmax=float(np.max(H)); spread=hmax-hmin
         cands=[]
         for frac in (0.1,0.2,0.4):
            thr=hmin + frac*spread
            b=(H<=thr).astype(float)
            bsum=b.sum(); if bsum>0: b/=bsum; cands.append(b)
         inv=1.0/(H+1e-15); isum=inv.sum(); if isum>0: cands.append(inv/isum)
         diff=np.maximum(hmax-H,0.0); dsum=diff.sum(); if dsum>0: cands.append(diff/dsum)
         # random low
         q=max(5, n//12)
         idx=np.argpartition(H, min(q,n-1))[:q]
         b=np.zeros(n); b[idx]=1.0; b/=q; cands.append(b)
         this_improved=False
         for b in cands:
            for mix in (0.2,0.4,0.6):
               now=time.monotonic(); if now>deadline-3: break
               a2=(1.0-mix)*a + mix*b
               a2=np.maximum(a2,0.0); s=a2.sum()
               if s<=0: continue
               a2/=s
               sc=_local_score(a2)
               if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                   best_score=sc; best=a2.copy(); a=a2.copy(); this_improved=True; improved_tau+=1
                   break
            if this_improved: break
         if not this_improved and it > 2:
             if improved_tau==0: break
   return best, best_score
```

```python
"""
Search for nonnegative step-function coefficients minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / sum(a)^2.

Strategy:
1. Start from any previously known sequence if present, plus many diverse
   low-dimensional antisymmetric sinusoidal / smooth-modulation candidates.
2. Optimize those low-dimensional candidates with LSE-smoothed max-convolution
   and L-BFGS-B.
3. Continue with full-sequence L-BFGS-B in a softmax parametrization using the
   exact FFT-based gradient of a log-sum-exp smoothing of the convolution peak.
4. Add a heuristic active-set / dual-sensitive mixing polish to escape shallow
   local minima.
All gradient and objective evaluations use FFT convolution; the final
candidate is checked against the imported evaluate_sequence() if available.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_TIME_STOP_DEADLINE = None
_FULL_STATE = None
_LOW_STATE = None


def _time_callback(*args):
    dl = _TIME_STOP_DEADLINE
    if dl is None:
        return False
    return time.monotonic() >= dl


def _png(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _softmax(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    if x.size == 0:
        return x
    y = x - x.max()
    e = np.exp(y)
    s = e.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones_like(x, dtype=float) / float(x.size)
    return e / s


def _conv_fft(a, N=None):
    n = int(a.size)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if N is None:
        N = _png(L)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N=N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, n=N)[:L]
    if c.size and c.min() < 0.0:
        c = np.where(c < 0.0, 0.0, c)
    return c


def _grad_weights(w, a, N=None):
    n = int(a.size)
    if N is None:
        N = _png(3 * n - 2)
    wr = w[::-1]
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N=N)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(wr, N=N)
    q = np.fft.irfft(fa * fw, n=N)
    idx = np.arange(2 * n - 2, n - 2, -1)
    return q[idx]


def _smooth_val_weights(c, tau):
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if tau <= 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros_like(c, dtype=float)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return m, w
    sh = (c - m) / tau
    e = np.exp(sh)
    s = e.sum()
    w = e / s
    return m + tau * float(np.log(s)), w


def _local_score_fast(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return float("inf")
    c = _conv_fft(a)
    m = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
    if m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * a.size * m / (s * s))


def _local_score_safe(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    a = np.minimum(a, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        return float("inf")
    c = _conv_fft(a)
    m = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
    if m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * a.size * m / (s * s))


def _normalize_nonnegative(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    a = np.minimum(a, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return None
    return a / s


class _FullState:
    def __init__(self, n):
        self.n = int(n)
        self.tau = 1.0 / max(1, n)
        self.cache_x = None
        self.cache_valid = False
        self.cache_val = float("inf")
        self.cache_grad = None


class _FourierState:
    def __init__(self, n, K):
        self.n = int(n)
        self.K = int(K)
        self.tau = 1.0 / max(1, n)
        i = np.arange(self.n, dtype=float)
        ms = np.arange(1, self.K + 1, dtype=float)
        self.S = np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * ms[:, None] * i[None, :] / float(self.n))
        self.S -= self.S.mean(axis=1, keepdims=True)
        self.cache_x = None
        self.cache_valid = False
        self.cache_val = float("inf")
        self.cache_grad = None


def _full_compute(st, x):
    xs = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    xs = np.where(np.isfinite(xs), xs, 0.0)
    if xs.size != st.n:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(st.n, dtype=float)
    if st.cache_x is not None and st.cache_valid and np.array_equal(st.cache_x, xs):
        return st.cache_val, st.cache_grad

    xw = xs - float(np.mean(xs))
    a = _softmax(xw)
    n = a.size
    Nc = _png(2 * n - 1)
    Ng = _png(3 * n - 2)

    c = _conv_fft(a, Nc)
    val, w = _smooth_val_weights(c, st.tau)
    ga = 2.0 * _grad_weights(w, a, Ng)
    dot = float(np.dot(a, ga))
    gx = st.n * a * (ga - dot)
    gx[~np.isfinite(gx)] = 0.0

    st.cache_x = xs.copy()
    st.cache_valid = True
    st.cache_val = float(st.n * val)
    st.cache_grad = gx
    return st.cache_val, gx


def _full_fun(x):
    st = _FULL_STATE
    if st is None:
        return 0.0
    v, _ = _full_compute(st, x)
    return v


def _full_jac(x):
    st = _FULL_STATE
    if st is None:
        return np.zeros(1, dtype=float)
    _, g = _full_compute(st, x)
    return g


def _lowdim_compute(st, x):
    xs = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    xs = np.where(np.isfinite(xs), xs, 0.0)
    if xs.size != st.K + 1:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(st.K + 1, dtype=float)
    if st.cache_x is not None and st.cache_valid and np.array_equal(st.cache_x, xs):
        return st.cache_val, st.cache_grad

    K = st.K
    n = st.n
    c = xs[:K]
    scale = float(np.clip(xs[K], 0.0, 4.0))

    u = st.S.T @ c
    t = np.tanh(u)
    praw = 1.0 + scale * t
    p = np.maximum(praw, 0.0)
    P = float(p.sum())

    if P <= 1e-14:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=float) / float(n)
        out = np.zeros(K + 1, dtype=float)
    else:
        a = p / P
        Nc = _png(2 * n - 1)
        Ng = _png(3 * n - 2)
        cconv = _conv_fft(a, Nc)
        val, w = _smooth_val_weights(cconv, st.tau)
        ga = 2.0 * _grad_weights(w, a, Ng)

        dot = float(np.dot(ga, p))
        grad_p = ga / P - dot / (P * P)

        out = np.empty(K + 1, dtype=float)
        pos = p > 1e-14
        if scale > 0.0:
            weight = grad_p * (scale * (1.0 - t * t))
            weight[~pos] = 0.0
            out[:K] = st.S @ weight
        else:
            out[:K] = 0.0
        if np.any(pos):
            out[K] = float(np.dot(grad_p[pos], t[pos]))
        else:
            out[K] = 0.0
        out *= float(st.n)
        val = st.n * val
        out[~np.isfinite(out)] = 0.0

    st.cache_x = xs.copy()
    st.cache_valid = True
    st.cache_val = float(val)
    st.cache_grad = out
    return st.cache_val, out


def _lowdim_fun(x):
    st = _LOW_STATE
    if st is None:
        return 0.0
    v, _ = _lowdim_compute(st, x)
    return v


def _lowdim_jac(x):
    st = _LOW_STATE
    if st is None:
        return np.zeros(1, dtype=float)
    _, g = _lowdim_compute(st, x)
    return g


def _lowdim_initializations(K, rng):
    inits = []
    modes = [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 7, 11, 17, 23, 29, 37, 47, 53, 64]
    for f in modes:
        if f <= K:
            for amp in (0.4, 0.8, 1.4, 2.0):
                for sc in (0.4, 1.0, 1.8, 2.6):
                    z = np.zeros(K + 1, dtype=float)
                    z[f - 1] = amp
                    z[K] = sc
                    inits.append(z)

    ms = np.arange(1, K + 1, dtype=float)
    decay = 1.0 / np.sqrt(ms)

    for _ in range(28):
        c = rng.standard_normal(K) * decay * rng.uniform(0.5, 2.2)
        z = np.zeros(K + 1, dtype=float)
        z[:K] = c
        z[K] = rng.uniform(0.4, 2.6)
        inits.append(z)

    for _ in range(16):
        z = np.zeros(K + 1, dtype=float)
        hi = min(16, K + 1)
        if hi <= 3:
            hi = K + 1
        k = int(rng.integers(3, hi))
        idx = rng.choice(K, k, replace=False)
        z[idx] = rng.choice([-1.0, 1.0], size=k) * rng.uniform(0.5, 1.8, size=k)
        z[K] = rng.uniform(0.5, 2.8)
        inits.append(z)

    rng.shuffle(inits)
    return inits


def _add_top(top, score, seq, max_len=5):
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return
    ins = False
    for i, (s, _) in enumerate(top):
        if score < s:
            top.insert(i, (score, seq.copy()))
            ins = True
            break
    if not ins:
        top.append((score, seq.copy()))
    if len(top) > max_len:
        top.pop()


def _lowdim_search(n, K, deadline, seed, max_starts=24):
    global _LOW_STATE, _TIME_STOP_DEADLINE
    st = _FourierState(n, K)
    _LOW_STATE = st
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    inits = _lowdim_initializations(K, rng)
    inits = inits[:max_starts]

    top = []
    bounds = [(-8.0, 8.0)] * K + [(0.0, 3.5)]
    taus = [4.0, 2.0, 1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.12, 0.06, 0.03]
    opts = {
        "maxiter": 1200,
        "maxfun": 2500,
        "ftol": 1e-12,
        "gtol": 1e-8,
        "maxls": 30,
    }

    for idx, x0 in enumerate(inits):
        for j, td in enumerate(taus):
            now = time.monotonic()
            remaining = deadline - now - 10.0
            if remaining <= 0.0:
                break
            rem_runs = max(1, (len(inits) - idx) * len(taus) - j)
            stage = remaining / float(rem_runs)
            stage = max(3.0, min(45.0, stage))
            if stage > remaining:
                stage = min(45.0, max(3.0, 0.9 * remaining))
            _TIME_STOP_DEADLINE = now + stage

            st.tau = td / float(n)
            try:
                res = minimize(
                    _lowdim_fun,
                    x0,
                    jac=_lowdim_jac,
                    method="L-BFGS-B",
                    bounds=bounds,
                    options=opts,
                    callback=_time_callback,
                )
                if res.x is not None:
                    x0 = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
            except Exception:
                pass

            a = _lowdim_a(x0, st)
            sc = _local_score_fast(a)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                _add_top(top, sc, a)
                if idx < 10 or sc < 1.52:
                    print(
                        f"lowdim n={n} init={idx} tau={td:.3f} score={sc:.6f}"
                    )
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5.0:
            break

    return top


def _lowdim_a(x, st):
    xs = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    xs = np.where(np.isfinite(xs), xs, 0.0)
    c = xs[: st.K]
    scale = float(np.clip(xs[st.K], 0.0, 4.0))
    u = st.S.T @ c
    t = np.tanh(u)
    p = np.maximum(1.0 + scale * t, 0.0)
    s = p.sum()
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(st.n, dtype=float) / float(st.n)
    return p / s


def _full_optimize(a0, deadline):
    global _FULL_STATE, _TIME_STOP_DEADLINE
    a = _normalize_nonnegative(a0)
    if a is None:
        a = np.ones(4096, dtype=float) / 4096
    n = a.size
    st = _FullState(n)
    _FULL_STATE = st

    x = np.log(a + 1e-14)
    x -= x.mean()
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = _local_score_fast(a)

    taus = [4.0, 2.0, 1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.12, 0.06, 0.03]
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    max_stages = max(1, min(len(taus), int(remaining / 70.0)))
    max_stages = min(max_stages, len(taus))
    opts = {
        "maxiter": 2500,
        "maxfun": 5000,
        "ftol": 1e-12,
        "gtol": 1e-9,
        "maxls": 40,
    }

    for j in range(max_stages):
        now = time.monotonic()
        remaining = deadline - now - 10.0
        if remaining <= 30.0:
            break
        stage = remaining / float(max_stages - j)
        stage = max(25.0, min(300.0, stage))
        if stage > remaining:
            stage = max(25.0, 0.9 * remaining)

        st.tau = taus[j] / float(n)
        _TIME_STOP_DEADLINE = now + stage
        try:
            res = minimize(
                _full_fun,
                x,
                jac=_full_jac,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                options=opts,
                callback=_time_callback,
            )
            if res.x is not None:
                x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
        except Exception:
            pass

        a = _softmax(x)
        sc = _local_score_fast(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_a = a.copy()
        print(f"full n={n} tau={taus[j]:.3f} score={best_score:.6f}")

    return best_a, best_score


def _active_mix_polish(a, deadline):
    a = _normalize_nonnegative(a)
    if a is None:
        a = np.ones(4096, dtype=float) / 4096
    n = a.size
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = _local_score_fast(a)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(987654321)

    taus = [3.0, 1.5, 0.75, 0.375, 0.1875, 0.09375]
    for td in taus:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5.0:
            break
        tau = td / float(n)
        improved_this_tau = 0
        for it in range(10):
            now = time.monotonic()
            if now > deadline - 3.0:
                break
            c = _conv_fft(a)
            _, w = _smooth_val_weights(c, tau)
            H = _grad_weights(w, a)
            H = np.where(np.isfinite(H), H, 0.0)
            hmin = float(H.min())
            hmax = float(H.max())
            spread = max(1e-18, hmax - hmin)

            cands = []

            for frac in (0.10, 0.22, 0.45):
                thr = hmin + frac * spread
                b = (H <= thr).astype(float)
                bs = b.sum()
                if bs > 0.0:
                    cands.append(b / bs)

            inv = 1.0 / (H + 1e-16)
            isum = inv.sum()
            if isum > 0.0:
                cands.append(inv / isum)

            diff = np.maximum(hmax - H, 0.0)
            dsum = diff.sum()
            if dsum > 0.0:
                cands.append(diff / dsum)

            q = max(1, min(n, n // 12))
            idx = np.argpartition(H, min(q, n - 1))[:q]
            b = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
            b[idx] = 1.0
            b /= float(q)
            cands.append(b)

            changed = False
            for b in cands:
                for mix in (0.18, 0.34, 0.55):
                    now = time.monotonic()
                    if now > deadline - 3.0:
                        break
                    a2 = (1.0 - mix) * a + mix * b
                    a2 = np.maximum(a2, 0.0)
                    s2 = float(a2.sum())
                    if s2 <= 0.0:
                        continue
                    a2 /= s2
                    sc = _local_score_fast(a2)
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                        best_score = sc
                        best_a = a2.copy()
                        a = a2.copy()
                        changed = True
                        improved_this_tau += 1
                        break
                if changed:
                    break

            if not changed and it > 2 and improved_this_tau == 0:
                break

    return best_a, best_score


def _get_initial_sequence():
    if "height_sequence_1" not in globals():
        return None
    base = height_sequence_1
    arr = np.asarray(base, dtype=float)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.minimum(arr, 1000.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        return None
    return arr / s


def propose_candidate():
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + 2340.0  # leave 60 seconds before the hard 2400s limit

    base = _get_initial_sequence()
    if base is not None:
        n = int(base.size)
        if n < 512:
            n = 4806
        elif n > 20000:
            n = 12000
    else:
        n = 4806

    best_a = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    if base is not None:
        base_score = _local_score_safe(base)
        if np.isfinite(base_score):
            best_a = base.copy()
            best_score = base_score
            print(f"initial height_sequence_1 length={base.size} score={best_score:.7f}")

    uniform = np.ones(n, dtype=float) / float(n)
    uniform_score = _local_score_fast(uniform)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score) or uniform_score < best_score:
        best_score = uniform_score
        best_a = uniform.copy()
        print(f"uniform n={n} score={best_score:.7f}")

    # Low-dimensional diverse constructions.
    lowdim_top = []
    if deadline - time.monotonic() > 150.0:
        lowdim_end = min(deadline - 300.0, start + 900.0)
        if lowdim_end > time.monotonic() + 45.0:
            lowdim_top = _lowdim_search(n, 64, lowdim_end, seed=20240516, max_starts=24)
            for sc, seq in lowdim_top:
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_a = seq.copy()

    # Prepare optimized-length candidates for full refinement.
    cands = []
    if base is not None and base.size == n:
        cands.append((_local_score_safe(base), base.copy()))
    else:
        cands.append((_local_score_fast(best_a), best_a.copy()))
    for sc, seq in lowdim_top:
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            cands.append((sc, seq.copy()))

    cands = [(s, a) for s, a in cands if np.isfinite(s)]
    cands.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])

    # One modest active-set polish before full L-BFGS to improve starting points.
    if deadline - time.monotonic() > 700.0:
        pre_active_end = min(deadline - 420.0, time.monotonic() + 240.0)
        if pre_active_end > time.monotonic() + 45.0:
            best_a, best_score = _active_mix_polish(best_a, pre_active_end)
            # Add polished best back into candidate list.
            cands.append((best_score, best_a.copy()))
            cands.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])

    # Full-sequence L-BFGS on the best few sequences.
    full_deadline = max(time.monotonic() + 60.0, deadline - min(300.0, max(120.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.2)))
    if full_deadline > time.monotonic() + 45.0 and cands:
        # Limit to at most three distinct-ish candidates.
        seen = []
        top_full = []
        for sc, seq in cands:
            dup = False
            for _, s2 in seen:
                if s2.shape == seq.shape and np.allclose(seq, s2, rtol=0.0, atol=1e-14):
                    dup = True
                    break
            if dup:
                continue
            top_full.append((sc, seq))
            seen.append((sc, seq))
            if len(top_full) >= 3:
                break

        remaining_full = full_deadline - time.monotonic()
        cnt = len(top_full)
        if remaining_full > 60.0:
            slice_time = remaining_full / float(cnt) if cnt else remaining_full
            for idx, (sc, seq) in enumerate(top_full):
                remaining = full_deadline - time.monotonic()
                if remaining < 45.0:
                    break
                alloc = min(full_deadline, time.monotonic() + slice_time)
                seq2, sc2 = _full_optimize(seq, alloc)
                if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score:
                    best_score = sc2
                    best_a = seq2.copy()
                cands.append((sc2, seq2.copy()))
                cands.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])

    # Final active-set polish.
    if deadline - time.monotonic() > 40.0:
        best_a, best_score = _active_mix_polish(best_a, deadline - 3.0)

    # Final safe normalization.
    final = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=float)
    if final.size == 0:
        final = uniform.copy()
    final = np.where(np.isfinite(final), final, 0.0)
    final = np.maximum(final, 0.0)
    final = np.minimum(final, 1000.0)
    s = float(final.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        final = np.ones(n, dtype=float) / float(n)
        s = 1.0
    final = final / s

    out_list = [float(v) for v in final]

    try:
        if "evaluate_sequence" in globals():
            off = evaluate_sequence(out_list)
            print(f"final official score={off:.7f} local={best_score:.7f} n={final.size}")
        else:
            print(f"final local score={best_score:.7f} n={final.size}")
    except Exception as exc:
        print("official evaluation skipped:", exc)

    return out_list
```
